2026-06-03 | ingest-pt2 | 1 net-new _raw drop post-pt1-merge: `_raw/A Functional Taxonomy of World Models.md` (Fei-Fei Li + World Labs team essay, published 2026-06-03 via X + Substack). No additional Daily Brief landed (latest is `Daily Briefs/2026-06-03.md` already processed in pt1). **One source page**: [[feifei-li-world-models-functional-taxonomy-2026-06-03]] — Fei-Fei Li + World Labs team's **first wiki-captured load-bearing taxonomy of world models**. Decomposes the heavily-overloaded "world model" term into **three functional projections of the Sutton & Barto POMDP agent ↔ world loop**: **renderer** (outputs pixels; visual-fidelity contract; Google Genie 3, World Labs RTFM, Nano Banana), **simulator** (outputs state + geometry + physics + dynamics; structural-accuracy contract; NVIDIA Omniverse, World Labs Marble, physics engines), **planner** (outputs actions; VLA models, model-based RL, World Action Models). **Thesis: simulation is the linchpin** — *"the structural backbone from which both visual appearance (for renderers) and action consequences (for planners) can be derived"*; renderer is commercially mature but cannot be trusted to design buildings or train robots; planner is the most nascent (demos confined to constrained labs); **simulator is the bridge**. **NVIDIA Omniverse ">$1T addressable market" framing** for the simulator category — first wiki-captured concrete-TAM framing for simulator as distinct from renderer. **First wiki-captured concrete Marble input/output schema**: multimodal inputs (text + image + video + spatial-sketch) → dual outputs (Gaussian splats for visual exploration / renderer-side + collision meshes for physics engines / simulator-side). Marble *"dissolves the boundary between the renderer and the simulator"* — first concrete instance of category-blending. **Logical endpoint**: unified world model — *"one foundation model that can render photorealistic views, produce physically accurate structure, and plan action sequences."* Open problem: *"reconciling these tensions inside a single architecture is the defining open problem in world model research today."* **Pattern**: *"every level is moving from passive output to interactive system — renderers becoming action-conditioned, simulators generating worlds that are more controllable and editable, planners deliberating rather than just reacting."* Historical anchoring: Kenneth Craik 1943 *"small-scale models"* + Sutton & Barto canonical POMDP picture + late-1980s/early-1990s neural-network adoption of the term. **New entity page**: [[marble]] created as standalone tools-page for World Labs Marble. **Cross-cutting synthesis**: (a) **First wiki-captured same-week 2-vendor convergence on the renderer-becomes-interactive direction** — xAI Grok Imagine ([[latentspace-video-agents-xai-grok-imagine-2026-06-02|Jun 02 ship]]) is making the renderer action-conditioned; World Labs is dissolving the renderer-simulator boundary. Both vendors driving toward the *"every-level-moving-from-passive-to-interactive"* pattern from different starting categories. (b) **Pairs structurally with [[lecun-path-autonomous-machine-intelligence|LeCun JEPA paper]]** — LeCun's framing addresses *what the architectural priors should be* (latent-space prediction; no generative pixel/token prediction); Fei-Fei's framing addresses *what the consumer-facing categories should be* (renderer + simulator + planner output projections). **Complementary, not contradictory** — both anchor the world-models concept page. (c) **Pairs with [[ineffable-intelligence|Silver RL-from-self-play]] + [[physical-intelligence|Physical Intelligence]] + [[ami-labs|AMI Labs]] + [[luma-ai|Luma AI Uni-1]]** — Fei-Fei's 3-category framework gives the **first wiki-captured load-bearing organizing-frame** to compare these adjacent lab bets *by which category they're producing* (Silver = planner-side via RL; PI = planner-side via foundation model; AMI/LeCun = renderer/simulator/planner via JEPA; Luma = renderer + simulator via unified latent space). (d) **Implicit Marble vs Omniverse competitive framing** — World Labs is positioned to compete in the simulator-side that NVIDIA Omniverse dominates, with AI-generative-from-multimodal-prompt as the differentiation rather than hand-authored 3D pipeline. (e) **Notably-absent**: no mention of Anthropic / OpenAI / DeepMind as world-model-vendors in the framing (Genie 3 and Nano Banana cited as renderer examples but not as world-model competitors). Fei-Fei is framing **World Labs as the simulator-side player** without engaging with frontier-lab branding. **Judgment calls**: did NOT create entity pages for Wittgenstein, Kenneth Craik, Sutton/Barto — single-historical-reference mentions; did NOT primary-fetch the Substack version of the essay (X version captured was sufficient); did NOT promote the *"From Words to Worlds: Spatial Intelligence"* earlier essay (referenced as background; flagged for follow-up ingest); did NOT propagate NVIDIA Omniverse ">$1T TAM" framing as wiki-canonical (single-source today; pattern-watch for NVIDIA-side confirmation); DID create new tools page [[marble]] at 2+-source threshold (cited in [[bloomberg-feifei-li-2025]] + now in [[feifei-li-world-models-functional-taxonomy-2026-06-03]]). **Verification-pending claims flagged inline**: Marble user / customer / revenue metrics; Marble model architecture + training data composition; NVIDIA Omniverse TAM source (NVIDIA filings? analyst report? internal estimate?); World Labs essay co-author identities (essay credits "World Labs team" without names); whether World Labs publishes a paper or remains at essay-level positioning; whether World Labs has signed NVIDIA Omniverse partner programs.
